# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-28)

## Corpus Check
- 4499 files · ~10,632,650 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 59888 nodes · 156576 edges · 3204 communities (1472 shown, 1732 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4401 edges (avg confidence: 0.72)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness

- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
